## Western Grain Transition Payments Regulations {#western-grain-transition-payments-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/95-314
:::

[WESTERN GRAIN TRANSITION PAYMENTS ACT](/eng/acts/W-7.8)

Registration 1995-06-29

Regulations Respecting Transition Payments Made to Owners of Farmland in
Western Canada on Which Grain is Grown

P.C. 1995-1092  1995-06-29
::::

::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Agriculture and Agri-Food, pursuant to section 6 of the
[Western Grain Transition Payments Act](/eng/acts/W-7.8)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_1e){#fn_1e-ID0EDBAA}, is pleased hereby to
make the annexed [Regulations respecting transition payments made to
owners of farmland in Western Canada on which grain is
grown](/eng/regulations/SOR-95-314).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_1e-ID0EDBAA){#fn_1e}S.C. 1995, c. 17, Sch.
  II
  :::
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-973575 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations may be
cited as the [Western Grain Transition Payments
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-95-314).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-973577 .Part}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Western Grain Transition
    Payments Act](/eng/acts/W-7.8); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[apportionment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[apportionment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the percentage of all
    transition payments that is apportioned to each part of the eligible
    area set out in subsection 4(5) of the Act;
    ([quote-part]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Creston-Wynndel Areas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Creston-Wynndel Areas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that part of
    British Columbia bounded by the boundary with the United States to
    the South, Duck Lake to the North, the Selkirk Mountains to the West
    and the Purcell Mountains to the East; ([régions de
    Creston-Wynndel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[distance factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[distance factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the factor referred to
    in section 9; ([facteur de distance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[dryland acres]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dryland acres]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the acres of an
    applicant's farmland that were not irrigated acres in 1994; ([acres
    non irriguées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[dryland productivity factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dryland productivity factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the factor
    determined in accordance with section 7; ([facteur de productivité
    des terres non irriguées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[equitable arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[equitable arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement
    between an owner of farmland that is the subject of an application
    for a transition payment and the lessee of that farmland in respect
    of the transition payment; ([entente équitable]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[final payment factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[final payment factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the factor
    determined in accordance with section 12; ([facteur du second
    versement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[interim payment factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[interim payment factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the factor
    determined in accordance with section 10; ([facteur du premier
    versement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[irrigated acres]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[irrigated acres]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the acres of an
    applicant's farmland that in 1994 were subject to sufficient
    irrigation by artificial means so that water was not a limiting
    factor in crop production; ([acres irriguées]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[irrigation productivity factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[irrigation productivity factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    factor determined in accordance with section 8; ([facteur de
    productivité des terres irriguées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[lessee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lessee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of farmland that is the
    subject of an application for a transition payment, means a person
    who, on August 1, 1995, holds

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a lease from the owner of the farmland; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of farmland in a reserve within the
      meaning of subsection 2(1) of the [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5),

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a permit to use that farmland pursuant to
        subsection 28(2) of that Act, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a lease of that farmland pursuant to
        subsection 53(1) or section 58 of that Act;
        ([preneur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[part of the eligible area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[part of the eligible area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Manitoba,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Saskatchewan, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Alberta and those parts of British Columbia known
      as the Peace River District and the Creston-Wynndel Areas;
      ([partie de la région désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Peace River District]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Peace River District]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means that part of
    British Columbia bounded by the boundary with Alberta to the East,
    with the Northwest Territories and the Yukon Territory to the North
    and the Rocky Mountains to the West and South. ([district de Peace
    River]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

## [Ownership]{.HTitleText1} {#h-973600 .Part}

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of the
definition [[own]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 2 of the Act, the
following interests, which were in existence at the beginning of the day
on February 28, 1995, are interests in farmland:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a contract, court order or any other legal instrument
  under which a person is entitled to become the holder of the fee
  simple in farmland;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a contract that is contingent on conditions being
  fulfilled to give effect to the contract, under which a person will be
  entitled to become the holder in fee simple in farmland, where those
  conditions are fulfilled by June 1, 1996;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an interest in farmland in a reserve held by an
  Indian band within the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the [Indian
  Act](/eng/acts/I-5), unless an Indian is in lawful possession of the
  farmland under paragraph (d);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an interest in farmland in a reserve held by an
  Indian who is in lawful possession of the farmland pursuant to
  subsection 20(1), (2) or (3), section 24, subsection 25(1), section 49
  or subsection 50(4) of the [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5); or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} an agreement of sale held by a person with The
  Director, The Veterans' Land Act, pursuant to the [Veterans' Land
  Act](/eng/acts/V-1.5).

## [Criteria for Entitlement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-973607 .Part}

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of subsection 4(1) of the Act, an applicant shall meet the
  following criteria:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant owns not less than ten acres of
    farmland located in the eligible area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant completes and signs an application
    form approved by the Minister;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} except as provided in paragraph (d), where the
    farmland that is the subject of an application is owned by more than
    one person, each owner signs the application;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} where the farmland that is the subject of an
    application is owned by an Indian band as set out in paragraph 3(c),
    the number of members that constitute a quorum of the council of the
    band within the meaning of subsection 2(1) of the [Indian
    Act](/eng/acts/I-5) sign the application;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsections (3) and (4), the applicant
    delivers or mails the application to the Minister no later than
    November 1, 1995; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} on request and no later than the date stipulated by
    the Minister, the applicant provides to the Minister any document or
    information relevant to the application.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister has received an application mailed
  or delivered in accordance with paragraph (1)(e), from a person other
  than the person who was the owner of the farmland at the beginning of
  the day on February 28, 1995, that owner may deliver or mail to the
  Minister, no later than March 1, 1996, an application in respect of
  that farmland.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application received by the Minister on or before
  February 29, 1996 shall be deemed to have been made in accordance with
  paragraph (1)(e) if it is accompanied by a statement, in a form
  acceptable to the Minister, that an equitable arrangement has been
  made between the applicant and the lessee of that applicant.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An application received by the Minister after
  February 29, 1996 but no later than April 1, 1997 shall be deemed to
  have been made in accordance with paragraph (1)(e) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no application in respect of the farmland has been
    received from any other applicant who was, at the beginning of the
    day on February 28, 1995, the holder of the fee simple in the
    farmland; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the application is accompanied by a statement, in a
    form acceptable to the Minister, that an equitable arrangement has
    been made between the applicant and the lessee of that applicant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-455, s. 1
- SOR/96-9, s. 1
- SOR/96-367, s. 1
- SOR/97-235, s. 1
:::

## [Conditions of Payment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-973623 .Part}

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the Minister has received an application in accordance with paragraph
  4(1)(e), a transition payment in respect of farmland leased by an
  applicant to a lessee shall be made, unless the Minister has received
  a letter, delivered or mailed on or before November 15, 1995, from the
  lessee stating that an equitable arrangement has not been made.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before a transition payment is made in respect of
  farmland leased by an applicant to a lessee where the Minister has
  received a letter referred to in subsection (1), the following
  conditions shall be met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on notification by the Minister of the receipt of
    the letter, the applicant shall deliver or mail to the Minister no
    later than May 1, 1996

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement, signed by both the applicant and the
      lessee of the farmland, stating that an equitable arrangement has
      been reached,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a binding decision of an arbitrator setting out
      the terms of the equitable arrangement, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a report of an arbitrator stating that the
      lessee failed to appear for arbitration or failed to pay a deposit
      required by an arbitrator; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where by March 1, 1996 no arbitrator has been
    agreed upon by the owner and the lessee, the owner shall

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} within 10 days after that date, select an
      arbitrator from a list of arbitrators supplied by the Minister,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} within 20 days after that date, give notice to
      the lessee of the time and place of the arbitration and of the
      lessee's one-half share of the arbitration fee, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} within 30 days after that date, deposit
      one-half of the arbitration fee which an arbitrator may require
      the owner and the lessee to deposit.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice referred to in subparagraph (2)(b)(ii)
  shall be sent to the lessee by registered mail with an acknowledgement
  of receipt attached at

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the address that the lessee set out in the letter
    referred to in subsection (2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the address of the lessee's last known residence or
    place of business where no address for the lessee is set out in the
    letter referred to in subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-455, s. 2
- SOR/96-9, s. 2
- SOR/96-367, s. 2
:::

## [Calculation of Transition Payments]{.HTitleText1} {#h-973640 .Part}

### [Interim Payment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-973641 .Subheading}

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  interim payment in respect of an applicant's farmland shall be
  calculated as follows:

  ::: {#973644 .Subsection}
  A × \[(B × C × D) + (E × F × D)\]

   where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the interim payment factor;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of dryland acres;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the dryland productivity factor;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the distance factor;

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of irrigated acres; and

  [F]{.dfn}
  :   is the irrigation productivity factor.
  :::

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the interim payment referred to in
  subsection (1) contains a fraction of a dollar, that fraction shall be
  expressed as a decimal fraction of three or more digits after the
  decimal point and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the third and subsequent digits after the decimal
    point shall be dropped if the third digit is less than five; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the second digit after the decimal point shall be
    increased by one and the third and subsequent digits dropped if the
    third digit is five or greater than five.

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** The dryland productivity
factor in respect of an applicant's farmland shall be determined using
the historic average crop insurance yields for wheat, barley and canola
on similar soils within a geographic area, such as a crop insurance risk
area, having similar levels of productivity, weighted by the proportions
of the acreage of wheat, barley, canola and summerfallow within a
geographic area, such as a rural municipality.

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** The irrigation productivity
factor in respect of an applicant's farmland shall be determined using
the historic average of the yields of wheat, barley and canola weighted
by the proportions of acreages of wheat, barley and canola on irrigated
acres of farmland in the part of the eligible area.

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), the distance factor in respect of an applicant's
  farmland shall be the number set out in column III of an item of the
  schedule for the station set out in column I of that item that is the
  closest station to both the centre of that farmland and the port set
  out in column II of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where because of a natural barrier or the location of
  a transportation route, the closest station to an applicant's farmland
  is not accessible, the Minister may substitute another station set out
  in column I of the schedule.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-367, s. 3
:::

### [Interim Payment Factor]{.HTitleText2} {#h-973662 .Subheading}

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  interim payment factor for the part of the eligible area in which an
  applicant's farmland is located shall not exceed but may be less than
  a factor calculated as follows:

  ::: {#973665 .Subsection}
  \$1,200,000,000 × A ÷ (Σ\[(B × C × D) + (E × F × D)\])

   where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the apportionment;

  [Σ]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of the sum of products calculated for farmland within
      the part of the eligible area for which an application has been
      received in accordance with paragraph 4(e);

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of dryland acres;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the dryland productivity factor;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the distance factor;

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of irrigated acres; and

  [F]{.dfn}
  :   is the irrigation productivity factor.
  :::

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the interim payment factor referred to in
  subsection (1) contains a fraction, that fraction shall be expressed
  as a decimal fraction of 10 or more digits after the decimal point and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tenth and subsequent digits after the decimal
    point shall be dropped if the tenth digit is less than five; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ninth digit after the decimal point shall be
    increased by one and the tenth and subsequent digits dropped if the
    tenth digit is five or greater than five.

### [Final Payment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-973677 .Subheading}

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  final payment in respect of an applicant's farmland shall be
  calculated as follows:

  ::: {#973680 .Subsection}
  {A × \[(B × C × D) + (E × F × D)\]} - G

   where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the final payment factor;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of dryland acres;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the dryland productivity factor;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the distance factor;

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of irrigated acres;

  [F]{.dfn}
  :   is the irrigation productivity factor; and

  [G]{.dfn}
  :   is the interim payment made to the applicant in respect of the
      applicant's farmland.
  :::

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the final payment referred to in subsection (1)
  contains a fraction of a dollar, that fraction shall be expressed as a
  decimal fraction of three or more digits after the decimal point and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the third and subsequent digits after the decimal
    point shall be dropped if the third digit is less than five; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the second digit after the decimal point shall be
    increased by one and the third and subsequent digits dropped if the
    third digit is five or greater than five.

### [Final Payment Factor]{.HTitleText2} {#h-973692 .Subheading}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  final payment factor for the part of the eligible area in which an
  applicant's farmland is located shall be calculated as follows:

  ::: {#973695 .Subsection}
  \$1,600,000,000 × A ÷ (Σ\[(B × C × D) + (E × F × D)\])

   where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the apportionment;

  [Σ]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of the sum of products calculated for the farmland of
      each applicant who meets the prescribed criteria and conditions in
      the part of the eligible area;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of dryland acres;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the dryland productivity factor;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the distance factor;

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of irrigated acres; and

  [F]{.dfn}
  :   is the irrigation productivity factor.
  :::

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the final payment factor referred to in
  subsection (1) contains a fraction, that fraction shall be expressed
  as a decimal fraction of 10 or more digits after the decimal point and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tenth and subsequent digits after the decimal
    point shall be dropped if the tenth digit is less than five; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ninth digit after the decimal point shall be
    increased by one and the tenth and subsequent digits dropped if the
    tenth digit is five or greater than five.
